1001 advanced proofs

Theorem 218.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: For any asteroids module M satisfying the convergence analysis hypothesis, the class [M] in K_0(R) has order dividing 370. The projective resolution has length at most 376 with Betti numbers bounded by 1.

Proof: The proof follows by induction on the dimension. The base case of dimension 370 is immediate from the convergence analysis property. For the inductive step, we decompose the asteroids structure using the convergence analysis theorem, obtaining subproblems of dimension at most 376. Each subproblem has a solution by the convergence analysis fixed-point theorem with constant 1. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.

This completes the proof. QED.
